2026-10-29T13:36:02.8027050Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve the PP4 bundle image again with the copy-engine wait fixed and probed, and the four-stage MegaMoE adopted by total saving

Run 40849459077 (build + PP4 serving of ba61a13) built and archived the
image (sha256 4c723944..., 12275482624 bytes) but the serving failed before
READY: on prefill-1 and prefill-2 the PP1 scheduler segfaulted in
cuStreamWaitEvent (Python frame: _qpp_recv_proxy -> forward_stream.wait_event
on the validation path) on the first warmup transfer; PP2 then lost its gloo
peer and the job ended with 137 at 6:52. No score.

1. Copy-engine proxy transport (patches/sglang/pp4-proxy-copy-engine.patch)
   - The receiver opened the sender's interprocess events with
     Event.from_ipc_handle(peer_device, h), i.e. in the sender device's
     context inside the receiver process, and then waited on them from its own
     device's forward stream. CUDA's simpleIPC opens imported events in the
     importing process's own device context and waits there; torch's
     rebuild_cuda_tensor also waits on a stream of the device it opened the
     event on. The patch now opens them on the receiving stage's own device;
     the handle message is only the list of IPC handles.
   - A segfault cannot be caught by the in-process validation/vote, so the
     copy engine is now opt-in: SGLANG_PP4_PROXY_TRANSPORT (default nccl) is
     read once; anything but "copy" keeps in-stream PyNccl with no IPC setup
     and no vote (the vote runs only in validate mode). All stages source the
     same prefill-kernels.env, so all or none exchange handles.
   - New in-job probe scripts/check-firmus-pp4-copy-engine.py (torchrun on the
     four Decode GPUs of the tail node, after the MoE input and Decode
     selections in the same background group, ~20 s, 60 s timeout, || true):
     the same reduce_tensor buffers, interprocess events, own-device
     from_ipc_handle, side-stream copies (a GPU sleep before every other copy
     so the wait must block), slot reuse gated by acks, exact comparison of
     PP4 row counts. --aggregate appends SGLANG_PP4_PROXY_TRANSPORT=copy only
     if all four ranks completed all 12 transfers with zero mismatches; a
     crash, timeout or mismatch keeps PyNccl. The in-serving 16-launch
     validation and vote still run on top when copy is enabled.

2. Prefill MegaMoE adoption rule (scripts/select-firmus-prefill-megamoe.py)
   In 40849459077 four-stage was bit-exact on all 24 GPU/routing cases and
   saved 1492 us of 64277 us (2.3%), but was rejected because two balanced
   cases measured +3.6 us and -12.9 us against a 13 us per-case margin, which
   is within the run-to-run noise of a single GPU. The rule is now: passed
   everywhere, not slower on any GPU (its two routings summed), and total
   saving >= the summed per-case margin. Replaying the archived results of
   40849459077: four-stage eligible (1492 us vs 321 us required), tail-split
   eligible (1330 us), four-stage wins -> DG_SM90_PREFILL_4STAGE=1.
   Expected effect: about -1.5 ms per PP4 stage period (MegaMoE ~64 ms of the
   ~124 ms period at the power cap; work removed, factor ~1).

3. Reuse the image built by 40849459077 (no rebuild)
   configs/firmus-prefill-pp4.json pins container_build_run_id 40849459077,
   sha256 4c7239444435963d1392dd8586df9e17b9690113fa207a556261300ccd2652eb,
   12275482624 bytes, source commit ba61a13; check-firmus-pp4-cpu.py
   PINNED_IMAGE follows. The image's build patches are unchanged; only
   runtime overlays and scripts changed since ba61a13.

Other in-job selections of 40849459077 (kept as they are): Prefill FA3 screen
-> n192-direct-o-varlen; Prefill MoE input -> fused (fused-gate below
margin); Decode overlays -> wide norm/quant (6.06 -> 2.51 us), SwapAB QKV/O
lost to the pinned DeepGEMM config (25.6 vs 23.1 us) and stay off.

Validation: the 26 overlays apply in configs/firmus-prefill-pp4.json order;
the PP4 CPU gate checks on the regenerated tree pass (direct loop with
validate/copy/fallback); full pytest suite and ruff pass; new tests for the
probe aggregate, its wiring, the patch default and the MegaMoE rule.

Judge by: score.json; results/pp4-copy-engine/selection.json and
"PP4 proxy transport: copy|nccl" in the Prefill logs; prefill-megamoe
selection (expect four-stage); PP0 batch period vs 124 ms of 40825417205;
#bootstrap-req; Decode #running-req and ITL p99.
